# Changes against the witnesses

Counts include substantive reading differences and changes in explicit uncertainty, not macro-only differences. Against C means the first run; the independent B15-02 second run is counted separately. Confidence columns count logged decisions, including retained doubts; these counts are not error rates. H-absent pages have no collation rows.

## Consequential corrections

- 13/4 first bigrading display, cancelled subscript: Retains cancelled index rather than suppressing its existence.
- 13/4 second bigrading, cancelled C subscript: Marks the second bigrading's C subscript as cancelled.
- 13/5 cancelled lower text: Restores live formes de R(G) and the cancelled original wording.
- 13/5 last prose phrase: Keeps the condition live; manuscript does not withdraw it.
- 13/5 symétrique: Symmetry is a live inserted requirement of (**).
- 13/6 end of upper NB: Prevents importing theorem words into the marginal note.
- 13/6 (i) définit: Keeps the asserted polarization property live.
- 13/6 (iii) leading insertion placement: Restores the scope of reductivity and the centralizer clause to (iii).
- 13/6 (iii) ambient group and cancellation: Preserves prime and identity-component index in the ambient group.
- 13/6 sideways (iii bis) group: Restores the adjoint quotient G^{ad}.
- 13/6 sideways (iii ter) sentence: Recovers finite-quotient reformulation omitted from H.
- 13/7 c) and homogeneous V insertion: Restores the order and quantifiers of the polarization condition.
- 13/7 polarization-form replacement: The bilinear-form requirement is live.
- 13/7 a) every point notation: Reads the quantifier over points, rather than inventing an extra V quantifier.
- 13/7 a) centralizer argument: Restores point parameter s in centralizer argument.
- 13/9 display α base point: The base-point glyph in α is now explicitly uncertain between xi and zeta; it is not silently standardized.
- 13/9 last formula: Preserves F(W) and the written product with barred S in place of H’s F(V) restricted to tilde S.
- 13/10 inline α: Retains the anomalous right-barred shaft in α rather than replacing it by a conventional arrow.
- 13/10 component insertion: Recovers the inserted restriction to one connected component S^i, absent from H.
- 13/12 subbundle definition: Recovers a subbundle P0 of P instead of H’s section of P0.
- 13/12 torsor condition: Restores the Γ_{0_S}-torsor and its natural action, both lost or altered in H.
- 13/12 modular topos: The manuscript says topos modulaire, not espace modulaire.
- 13/16 component annotation placement: Places “a connected component” on the inclusion into Q(R), rather than on the definition of Q.
- 13/17 functor specification: The inserted analytic parameter specification is live, not withdrawn.
- 13/17 group leaving V_0 fixed: The live field in the stabilizer sentence is R over an overwritten earlier character, not the clean Q of H.
- 13/17 modular-variety opening: H replaces an explicit expectation with different prose and loses the qualification morally.
- 13/17 Frobenius cancellation and membership: The live membership sign before I(Q) and the overwritten expression are retained.
- 13/18 positivity insertion extent: The explicit statement that the positivity condition is not written down is live.
- 13/18 weights sentence: H loses the torus, its overwritten base field, and the word operating.
- 13/19 relative motive sentence: The text explicitly says relative motive; H replaces the mathematical object and rewrites its base.
- 13/19 compact group: Only the lower R is cancelled; the page does not contain a cancelled duplicate of the entire group expression.
- 13/23 cancelled opening question: The full withdrawn question about whether the Theta operations determine structures 1 and 2 is recovered.
- 13/23 fonctorielle: Functoriality is live and underlined, not cancelled.
- 13/23 first displayed equation: Raised V is an object index, not a check accent; cancelled extra superscript is retained.
- 13/23 endomorphism target: The endomorphism target is live; bold retracing does not cancel it.
- 13/23 linearity assertion: The displayed map is said to be K-linear, not labelled K-derivations.
- 13/24 opening target: The opening target has lowercase k, although the following display has uppercase K.
- 13/24 interlinear formula: The insertion is the differential expression dω(X∧Y), not the word “dans”.
- 13/24 first descent display: Preserves the mixed uppercase K and lowercase k actually written in the first descent display.
- 13/24 descended form subscript: Restores the live subscript k of the descended form.
- 13/24 cancelled qui and inserted extension clause: Restores the condition on the scalar extension u_K and its placement.
- 13/25 opening subject: Restores the scalar-extension subscript on the first u.
- 13/25 N.B. opening: The N.B. concerns the Lie algebra generated by the subspace, not an operation on it.
- 13/27 formula (4) operator: Retains the marked Isom operator used for the torsor; H omits its lower stroke.
- 13/27 formula (8) description: Restores the tensor-automorphism qualification in the description of H′.
- 13/28 second group formula operator: Preserves the written circled-minus subscript instead of regularising it to tensor.
- 13/28 second line of brace parenthesis: The parenthesis explicitly says a left torsor (à g.), not a right torsor.
- 13/28 item f field letter: Introduces doubt at an agreed field letter: the ink looks closer to uppercase K than to the witnesses’ k.
- 13/29 inserted Hodge qualification: The inserted qualification says “sans structure de Hodge”, not “avec”.
- 13/29 live functor in first product: Preserves the written T_D in the first product.
- 13/29 first automorphism operator: Adds uncertainty to an agreed tensor subscript whose inner strokes are not secure.
- 13/29 condition 2: The second condition requires the Hodge object to be “motivique”, not “intrinsèque”.
- 13/29 condition 3 comparison target: Preserves the written T_D again in the condition 3 comparison.
- 13/29 last inserted hypothesis: The Hodge-conjecture hypothesis is a live underlined insertion, not a retracted statement.
- 13/30 conjugation display: Restores the conjugation of the Hodge components, replacing H’s unrelated psi inverse equation.
- 13/30 right factor in product isomorphism: Removes H’s extra prime on the multiplicative group.
- 13/30 complex product sign: Preserves the circled product sign written in the complexified expression.
- 13/30 functor compatibility: The compatibility phrase names i and epsilon; C’s tensor symbol is not in the ink.
- 13/31 use of Hodge conjecture: The appeal to the Hodge conjecture is live and underlined, not withdrawn.
- 13/31 first complexified-group relation: Retains explicit uncertainty between equality and inclusion in the first group relation.
